Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any deep module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 175. The projective resolution has length at most 903 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by deep generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 175, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 903. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
